-- M10RU Bypass
-- Clean Speed Bypass with Modern Square Layout, Animated Race Track Border, Starry Space Background, Colors Palette & Auto Use on Steal

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

-- Remove existing GUI
local existing = playerGui:FindFirstChild("M10RUBypass")
if existing then
    existing:Destroy()
end

-- Configuration & Storage
local CONFIG_FILE = "M10RUBypass_Config.json"
local config = {
    power = 100000,
    pcPower = 100000,
    mobilePower = 72000,
    deviceMode = "PC",
    keybind = nil,
    keybindType = nil,
    autoEnable = false,
    colorTheme = "purple",
}

pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then
        local raw = readfile(CONFIG_FILE)
        local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
        if type(data) == "table" then
            if data.power then config.power = data.power end
            if data.pcPower then config.pcPower = data.pcPower end
            if data.mobilePower then config.mobilePower = data.mobilePower end
            if data.deviceMode then config.deviceMode = data.deviceMode end
            if data.keybind ~= nil then config.keybind = data.keybind end
            if data.keybindType ~= nil then config.keybindType = data.keybindType end
            if data.autoEnable ~= nil then config.autoEnable = data.autoEnable end
            if data.colorTheme then config.colorTheme = data.colorTheme end
        end
    end
end)

local function saveConfig()
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(config))
        end
    end)
end

-- Color Themes Table (Pure Colors Without Names)
local THEMES = {
    purple = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(165, 80, 255),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(200, 130, 255),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(36, 14, 58),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(180, 95, 255),
    },
    red = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 65),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 95, 105),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(50, 10, 15),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 80),
    },
    pumpkin = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 25),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(50, 22, 6),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 145, 30),
    },
    blue = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(90, 215, 255),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(8, 28, 55),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(30, 185, 255),
    },
    black = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 95),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 16),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48),
    },
    green = {
        primary = Color3.fromRGB(40, 230, 90),
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 130),
        dark = Color3.fromRGB(10, 50, 20),
        glow = Color3.fromRGB(50, 240, 100),
    },
}

local currentTheme = THEMES[config.colorTheme] or THEMES.purple

local enabled = false
local connection = nil
local autoEnableState = nil
local listeningForKey = false

-- Core Bypass Engine (V1 RenderStepped lag engine)
local function stopV1()
    if connection then
        connection:Disconnect()
        connection = nil
    end
end

local function startV1()
    stopV1()
    connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not enabled then return end
        local delay = (config.power - 10000) / 490000 * 0.2
        local t = tick()
        while tick() - t < delay do end
    end)
end

local function setEnabled(state)
    enabled = state
    if enabled then
        startV1()
    else
        stopV1()
    end
end

-- ========================================================================
-- GUI CREATION
-- ========================================================================
local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
screenGui.Name = "M10RUBypass"
screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
screenGui.DisplayOrder = 9999
screenGui.Parent = playerGui

pcall(function()
    if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(screenGui) end
end)

-- Main Square Frame (230 x 230)
local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
mainFrame.Name = "MainSquare"
mainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 230)
mainFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(35, 180)
mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 7, 12)
mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
mainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
mainFrame.Active = true
mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

local mainCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
mainCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
mainCorner.Parent = mainFrame

-- Animated Space Stars Layer
local starContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
starContainer.Name = "StarSpaceContainer"
starContainer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
starContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
starContainer.ZIndex = 1
starContainer.ClipsDescendants = true
starContainer.Parent = mainFrame

local stars = {}
for i = 1, 35 do
    local s = Instance.new("Frame")
    s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.random(1, 3), math.random(1, 3))
    s.Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random())
    s.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.accent
    s.BorderSizePixel = 0
    s.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(20, 80) / 100
    s.ZIndex = 1
    s.Parent = starContainer
    Instance.new("UICorner", s).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    table.insert(stars, {
        frame = s,
        speedX = (math.random(-20, 20) / 100),
        speedY = (math.random(15, 45) / 100),
    })
end

-- Animated Racing Track Border (Continuous Spinning Glow)
local raceBorder = Instance.new("UIStroke")
raceBorder.Name = "RaceBorder"
raceBorder.Thickness = 2.4
raceBorder.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
raceBorder.Parent = mainFrame

local raceGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
raceGradient.Rotation = 0
raceGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentTheme.dark),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.42, currentTheme.primary),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.58, currentTheme.accent),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentTheme.dark),
})
raceGradient.Parent = raceBorder

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    raceGradient.Rotation = (raceGradient.Rotation + dt * 150) % 360
    for _, starData in ipairs(stars) do
        local pos = starData.frame.Position
        local newY = pos.Y.Scale + (starData.speedY * dt * 0.1)
        local newX = pos.X.Scale + (starData.speedX * dt * 0.1)
        if newY > 1 then newY = -0.05 newX = math.random() end
        if newX > 1 then newX = 0 elseif newX < 0 then newX = 1 end
        starData.frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(newX, newY)
    end
end)

-- Main Content Container (ZIndex = 3)
local content = Instance.new("Frame")
content.Name = "Content"
content.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
content.ZIndex = 3
content.Parent = mainFrame

-- Dragging Support with UI Lock
local uiLocked = false
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil

content.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if uiLocked then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = mainFrame.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if not uiLocked and dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)

local cachedLogoAsset = nil
local cachedBlackAsset = nil

local function getExactLogoAsset()
    if cachedLogoAsset then return cachedLogoAsset end
    local fileName = "m10ru_logo_exact.png"
    if not (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        if writefile then
            if crypt and crypt.base64decode then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, crypt.base64decode(M10RU_LOGO_DATA))
            elseif syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64 then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, syn.crypt.base64.decode(M10RU_LOGO_DATA))
            elseif base64_decode then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, base64_decode(M10RU_LOGO_DATA))
            end
        end
    end
    if getcustomasset and (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        cachedLogoAsset = getcustomasset(fileName)
    elseif getsynasset and (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        cachedLogoAsset = getsynasset(fileName)
    end
    return cachedLogoAsset
end

local function getBlackLogoAsset()
    if cachedBlackAsset then return cachedBlackAsset end
    local fileName = "m10ru_logo_black.png"
    if not (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        if writefile then
            if crypt and crypt.base64decode then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, crypt.base64decode(M10RU_BLACK_DATA))
            elseif syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64 then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, syn.crypt.base64.decode(M10RU_BLACK_DATA))
            elseif base64_decode then
                pcall(writefile, fileName, base64_decode(M10RU_BLACK_DATA))
            end
        end
    end
    if getcustomasset and (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        cachedBlackAsset = getcustomasset(fileName)
    elseif getsynasset and (isfile and isfile(fileName)) then
        cachedBlackAsset = getsynasset(fileName)
    end
    return cachedBlackAsset or getExactLogoAsset()
end

local topIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
topIcon.Name = "TopIcon"
topIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 106, 0, 36)
topIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
topIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6)
topIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
topIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
topIcon.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Default
topIcon.ZIndex = 5
topIcon.Parent = content

task.spawn(function()
    if config.colorTheme == "black" then
        topIcon.Image = getBlackLogoAsset()
        topIcon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    else
        topIcon.Image = getExactLogoAsset()
        topIcon.ImageColor3 = (config.colorTheme == "purple") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or currentTheme.primary
    end
end)

-- Lock Button (Unlocked / Locked) next to Settings Button
local lockBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
lockBtn.Name = "LockBtn"
lockBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
lockBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0, 12)
lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 14, 30)
lockBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
lockBtn.Text = "🔓"
lockBtn.TextColor3 = currentTheme.primary
lockBtn.TextSize = 13
lockBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
lockBtn.ZIndex = 6
lockBtn.Parent = content
Instance.new("UICorner", lockBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

local lockBtnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", lockBtn)
lockBtnStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
lockBtnStroke.Thickness = 1.2
lockBtnStroke.Transparency = 0.4

lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    uiLocked = not uiLocked
    if uiLocked then
        lockBtn.Text = "🔒"
        lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 18, 25)
        TweenService:Create(lockBtnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75)}):Play()
    else
        lockBtn.Text = "🔓"
        lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 14, 30)
        TweenService:Create(lockBtnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = currentTheme.primary}):Play()
    end
end)

toggleStatusBadge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20)

toggleStatusBadge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 15, 20)
toggleStatusBadge.Text = "OFF"
toggleStatusBadge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 85, 85)

local function updateToggleVisual()
    if enabled then
        toggleStatusBadge.Text = "ON"
        toggleStatusBadge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 120)
        toggleStatusBadge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 45, 25)
        toggleDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 120)
        dotGlow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 120)
        TweenService:Create(toggleBtnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = currentTheme.accent, Thickness = 2.2}):Play()
        toggleBtnGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(currentTheme.primary, currentTheme.dark)
    else
        toggleStatusBadge.Text = "OFF"
        toggleStatusBadge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 85, 85)
        toggleStatusBadge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 15, 20)
        toggleDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75)
        dotGlow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75)
        TweenService:Create(toggleBtnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = currentTheme.primary, Thickness = 1.8}):Play()
        toggleBtnGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(35, 20, 50), Color3.fromRGB(15, 10, 22))
    end
end

toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    setEnabled(not enabled)
    updateToggleVisual()
end)

mobileBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
mobileBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
mobileBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
mobileBtn.Text = "Mobile"
mobileBtn.TextColor3 = (config.deviceMode == "Mobile") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 185)
mobileBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
mobileBtn.TextSize = 12
mobileBtn.ZIndex = 7

local function updateDeviceButtons(animated)
    local targetPos = (config.deviceMode == "PC") and UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3) or UDim2.new(0.5, 1, 0, 3)
    if animated then
        TweenService:Create(modePill, TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Position = targetPos,
            BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.primary,
        }):Play()
    else
        modePill.Position = targetPos
        modePill.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    end

    pcBtn.TextColor3 = (config.deviceMode == "PC") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 185)
    mobileBtn.TextColor3 = (config.deviceMode == "Mobile") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 185)
    powerBox.Text = tostring(config.power)
end

pcBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if config.deviceMode ~= "PC" then
        config.deviceMode = "PC"
        config.power = config.pcPower
        updateDeviceButtons(true)
        saveConfig()
    end
end)

mobileBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if config.deviceMode ~= "Mobile" then
        config.deviceMode = "Mobile"
        config.power = config.mobilePower
        updateDeviceButtons(true)
        saveConfig()
    end
end)

powerBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
    local val = tonumber(powerBox.Text)
    if val and val > 0 then
        config.power = math.floor(val)
        if config.deviceMode == "PC" then
            config.pcPower = config.power
        else
            config.mobilePower = config.power
        end
        saveConfig()
    end
    powerBox.Text = tostring(config.power)
end)

-- Keybind interaction
keybindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    listeningForKey = true
    keybindBtn.Text = "..."
    keybindBtn.TextColor3 = currentTheme.accent
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if listeningForKey then
        if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                config.keybind = nil
                config.keybindType = nil
            else
                config.keybind = input.KeyCode.Name
                config.keybindType = "Keyboard"
            end
            listeningForKey = false
            keybindBtn.Text = config.keybind or "None"
            keybindBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            saveConfig()
            return
        end
    end

    if processed then return end
    if config.keybind and input.KeyCode and input.KeyCode.Name == config.keybind then
        setEnabled(not enabled)
        updateToggleVisual()
    end
end)

-- 3. Always-Open Horizontal Scrollable Palette (Big Solid Square Tiles, NO Text Labels)
local colorsScroller = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
colorsScroller.Name = "ColorsScroller"
colorsScroller.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 68)
colorsScroller.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 114)
colorsScroller.BackgroundTransparency = 1
colorsScroller.BorderSizePixel = 0
colorsScroller.ScrollBarThickness = 3
colorsScroller.ScrollBarImageColor3 = currentTheme.primary
colorsScroller.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
colorsScroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 6 * 68 + 8, 0, 0)
colorsScroller.ZIndex = 32
colorsScroller.Parent = settingsModal

local themeKeys = {"purple", "red", "pumpkin", "blue", "black", "green"}

local function applyColorTheme(themeKey)
    config.colorTheme = themeKey
    currentTheme = THEMES[themeKey] or THEMES.purple
    saveConfig()

    setBtnStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    settingsBtn.TextColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    keyCardStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    powerCardStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    toggleBtnStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    modeStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    modePill.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    setModalStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
    sTitle.TextColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    colorsScroller.ScrollBarImageColor3 = currentTheme.primary

    updateToggleVisual()

    if config.autoEnable then
        autoSwitchBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    end

    for _, star in ipairs(stars) do
        star.frame.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.accent
    end

    raceGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentTheme.dark),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.42, currentTheme.primary),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.58, currentTheme.accent),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentTheme.dark),
    })

    -- Ensure container backgrounds adapt to current theme dark tone
    modeContainer.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.dark
    toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentTheme.dark
    if not uiLocked then
        lockBtnStroke.Color = currentTheme.primary
        lockBtn.TextColor3 = currentTheme.primary
    end

    -- Switch to true black image or smoothly tint
    if topIcon then
        if themeKey == "black" then
            topIcon.Image = getBlackLogoAsset()
            TweenService:Create(topIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
                ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            }):Play()
        else
            topIcon.Image = getExactLogoAsset()
            local targetColor = (themeKey == "purple") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or currentTheme.primary
            TweenService:Create(topIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                ImageColor3 = targetColor
            }):Play()
        end
    end
end

-- Create Big Square Color Tiles (Solid Color Only, No Text)
for idx, tKey in ipairs(themeKeys) do
    local tData = THEMES[tKey]

    local tile = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tile.Name = "ColorTile_" .. tKey
    tile.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 58)
    tile.Position = UDim2.new(0, (idx - 1) * 68 + 4, 0, 4)
    tile.BackgroundColor3 = tData.primary
    tile.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tile.Text = "" -- No text inside the tile!
    tile.AutoButtonColor = false
    tile.ZIndex = 33
    tile.Parent = colorsScroller
    Instance.new("UICorner", tile).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

    local tileStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", tile)
    tileStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    tileStroke.Thickness = 1.2
    tileStroke.Transparency = 0.5

    tile.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        applyColorTheme(tKey)
        TweenService:Create(tile, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 62),
        }):Play()
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            TweenService:Create(tile, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 58),
            }):Play()
        end)
    end)
end

-- Open / Close Settings Modal
local settingsOpen = false
local function setSettingsVisible(visible)
    settingsOpen = visible
    if settingsOpen then
        settingsModal.Visible = true
        settingsModal.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        TweenService:Create(settingsModal, TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 200),
        }):Play()
    else
        local t = TweenService:Create(settingsModal, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        })
        t:Play()
        t.Completed:Connect(function()
            if not settingsOpen then settingsModal.Visible = false end
        end)
    end
end

settingsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    setSettingsVisible(not settingsOpen)
end)

sClose.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    setSettingsVisible(false)
end)

-- Auto Enable On Steal Watcher (Exact Logic from IrishHub)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not config.autoEnable then
        autoEnableState = nil
        return
    end
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    local ws = hum.WalkSpeed
    local stolen = ws >= 10 and ws < 25
    if autoEnableState == nil then
        autoEnableState = stolen
        return
    end
    if stolen and not autoEnableState then
        autoEnableState = true
        setEnabled(true)
        updateToggleVisual()
    elseif not stolen and autoEnableState then
        autoEnableState = false
        setEnabled(false)
        updateToggleVisual()
    end
end)

-- Initial State
updateDeviceButtons(false)
updateToggleVisual()
applyColorTheme(config.colorTheme or "purple")
